Invoke proactively before committing Terraform changes. - **tf-reviewer**: Recenzent planu Terraforma. Użyj po `terraform plan`, żeby ocenić koszt, bezpieczeństwo i zgodność z zasadami demo, zanim padnie `apply`. ([Let-s-Code-AI/user_template](https://github.com/Let-s-Code-AI/user_template/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tf-reviewer.md))
- **tf-team-reviewer**: Diff reviewer for parallel infra-build agent teams. Enforces quaternion-only internal math, REP-105 frame naming, MessageFilter for time-synchronized sensor fusion, and TF tree coherence. Runs 'ros2 run tf2_tools view_frames' to audit the live transform tree. Use for any spatial transform, sensor fusion, or frame algebra task. ([RiddheshMore/everything-ros2](https://github.com/RiddheshMore/everything-ros2/blob/HEAD/everything-ros2-claude-code/agents/tf2-cartographer.md))
- **tf2-engine-reader**: Read-only Source SDK quoter. Returns an engine function VERBATIM with file:line, plus every override of every virtual it calls, plus where its flags are set and cleared. ([PinKushin/Tf2DemoSalvage](https://github.com/PinKushin/Tf2DemoSalvage/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tf2-engine-reader.md) · ★1)
- **tf3-docs-researcher**: Answers Transport Fever 3 modding questions ("how do I…", "what fields does X take", "how does the base game do Y") by searching the local wiki mirror, Teal API definitions, and extracted game files. Use it before writing or changing any mod code. Read-only; returns answers with file citations. ([dylanfisher/tf3-mods](https://github.com/dylanfisher/tf3-mods/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tf3-docs-researcher.md))
- **tf3-docs-syncer**: Re-syncs the local TF3 documentation (wiki mirror and extracted game files) and reports what changed upstream or in a game update. Use it when the user asks to refresh or update the docs, after a game patch, or when the docs look stale or missing. ([dylanfisher/tf3-mods](https://github.com/dylanfisher/tf3-mods/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tf3-docs-syncer.md))
- **tf3-fork-merger**: Merges a newer upstream revision of a third-party mod into its local "SQ Opt" fork (mods/square_opt_*), keeping the fork's optimizations. Use it when `make fork-status` shows UPDATE AVAILABLE, or when the user asks to pull an author's update into a fork. Runs the three-way merge, resolves conflicts, verifies every change listed in the fork's FORK.md survived, runs check/test, and finishes the merge. ([dylanfisher/tf3-mods](https://github.com/dylanfisher/tf3-mods/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tf3-fork-merger.md))
- **tf3-mod-scaffolder**: Creates a new Transport Fever 3 mod in mods/&lt;modId&gt;/ with a correct mod.json, _metadata/modinfo.json and optional script stub, then adds starter content for the requested mod type (vehicle repaint, asset, construction, config change, script mod) by modeling it on official examples. Use it when the user wants to start a new mod. ([dylanfisher/tf3-mods](https://github.com/dylanfisher/tf3-mods/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tf3-mod-scaffolder.md))
- **tf3-mod-validator**: Reviews a Transport Fever 3 mod for upload readiness. Runs the automated checks in tools/validate_mod.py, then reviews the things automation can't check against the wiki guidelines and requirements. Use it before publishing or updating a mod, or when a mod fails to load. Read-only except for running checks. ([dylanfisher/tf3-mods](https://github.com/dylanfisher/tf3-mods/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/tf3-mod-validator.md))
- **tfc-deep**: Expert worker (Opus 5.5, high effort). - **tfc-fast**: Fast, cheap worker (Haiku 5.5, medium effort). - **tfc-max**: Frontier worker (Fable 5.2, high effort). Use only when a lower tier would likely fall short. Expensive. ([mittuled/tfc](https://github.com/mittuled/tfc/blob/HEAD/agents/tfc-max.md))
- **tfc-std**: Standard worker (Sonnet 5.5, medium effort). Use for well-specified coding, everyday writing and editing, data analysis, multi-source research and synthesis, and agentic tool use where the task is clear. ([mittuled/tfc](https://github.com/mittuled/tfc/blob/HEAD/agents/tfc-std.md))

([rohitg00/ai-engineering-from-scratch](https://github.com/rohitg00/ai-engineering-from-scratch/blob/HEAD/interviews/questions/agents/the-agent-loops-forever.md) · ★80k · 5 copies)
- **the-agent-loops-forever**: 值班工程师向你发来告警。一个原本通常只需十分钟的编码任务，某 agent 已经连续运行了整整两个小时。追踪日志显示：一条 shell 命令、一个执行结果，然后又是完全相同的同一条命令，如此反反复复循环往复。请逐步推演你将如何排查根因，以及你需要做哪些改造以彻底防止该问题重演。 ([fancyboi999/ai-engineering-from-scratch-zh](https://github.com/fancyboi999/ai-engineering-from-scratch-zh/blob/HEAD/interviews/questions/agents/the-agent-loops-forever.md) · ★1.4k)
